---------------
| Brief Intro |
---------------

Greetings all.  I've decided to create a FAQ for WWE SmackDown!: Here
Comes The Pain.  I thought that since school hasn't started up yet for
this spring, I might as well do something that'll occupy my time other
than work.

----------------
| Opening Menu |
----------------

This is the first menu you see when you finally get past all the copyright
information and game creator/producer stuff.

Exhibition
----------
This mode is purely for fun.  You can go solo or with up to five others.
Listed here are the following modes: single, tag, 6-man tag, handicap,
hardcore, royal rumble, survival, and main events.  I'll show what sub-
modes are listed within these further down in the FAQ.

Create
------
This is where you can create stuff.  Listed under this option are: create
an appearance, create a move set, create an animation, and create a
stable.  I'm not going to really go into these too much, but I'll provide
a little description of what they are a little further down in the FAQ.

Season
------
This is where you go through a year in the WWE, starting in April and
ending in March of the following year.  Some storylines, lots of areas
in which to fight in, and many superstars who are trying to take you down.
You can also raise the stats of your superstar and buy items to unlock
in the ShopZone with SD$.

Options
-------
This is where you can set match rules, in game options, and load your
system data.  When you're done here, you can save your changed options
and exit back to the opening menu.

Credits
-------
This is unlocked after you complete season mode the first time.

------------
| Controls |
------------

Menu Controls
    X: Confirm
    Triangle (T): Cancel/Go Back
    D-Pad: Highlight Selection

Game Controls
    D-Pad/Left Analog Stick: Move Character
    X: Attacks
    O: Irish Whip
    T: Run
    []: Pick Up Weapon/Remove Turnbuckle/Search For Weapon
    R1: Change Focus
    L1: Perform Finishing Move (must be in appropriate position)
    R2: Reverse Grapple Attacks
    L2: Reverse Striking Attacks
    Direction + O: Grapple
    R2 + L2: Reverse Finishing Move
    Right Analog Stick: Taunt
    Start: Pause Menu
    L2 + L1: Do opponent's finisher (must be in appropriate position)

Special Controls
----------------
Bra & Panties Match
  Down + O: Rip off opponents clothes (opponent on the ground)

Ladder Match
  []: Set Up Ladder (while holding ladder)/Knock Down Ladder
  Direction + []: Climb Ladder
  X: Aerial Attack (while on ladder)
  O: Reach For Belt

Table Match or any match that has a table in it
  Up/Down + X: Put Opponent On Table (while holding table)
  O: Grapple W/ Opponent (while opponent is on table)
  Direction + O: Grapple Move Through Table (while grappling on table)

Hell In A Cell
  T: Open Door (best if running into the door)
  T/[]: Exit Hell In A Cell

Hardcore Match
  []: Change Location (while standing around the blue triangle thing)

Brief Explanation
-----------------

Here's some info for a few of the matches.

Ultimate Submission is sort of like an Ironman match, but the difference
is that pin falls don't count.  Instead, you have to make your opponent
tap out, or submit, in order to add one to your win count.

For the Special Referee matches, you can choose to be the special referee.
However, take note that if you do, you also have the same stats as a one.
That is, if you get knocked down, you get back up like you were a ref,
very slowly.

In the Royal Rumble, you can choose which slot you'd like to come out in.
First, last, somewhere in between, or randomly selected.

The Elimination Chamber is a new addition to this game.  It's sort of like
the Royal Rumble except that there are only six entrants (the game can
only have six total characters on the screen at any given time) and four
of them are inside of chambers.  Two start in the ring and every two
minutes or so, another character is released from a randomly selected
chamber.

The Armageddon Hell In A Cell is the match where there are six characters
going against each other.  Basically, think of a Fatal 4-Way, but with two
more characters to fight against.

The Bra & Panties match is also another new feature to the game.  It's
limited to only the female characters, not including any of the created
characters you may have made.  You win by stripping your opponent down to
their bra and panties, hence the name of the match.

The TLC Title match works the same as a Ladder match.  The only
difference is there are a table and a chair.  But you still need to use
the ladder to retrieve the belt.

That should be about it.  Send me an email if you want me to explain any
of the other matches.

-----------
| Weapons |
-----------

Weapons are all over the place in the game.  If you're at the ring area,
on the left and right sides of the ring on the outside, you can search for
a steel chair.  At the north part of the ring, the side closest to the
ramp, you can find a table.  At the south part of the ring, the side
closest to the two announcer's tables, you can find a sledgehammer.

You can even use the two announcer's tables and not get DQ'ed.  Can't be
penalized for what's already there.

The steel steps can also be used as well, but they need to be knocked
apart first.  Also, if you whip your opponent the right way outside of the
ring, you can whip him/her into the steel post.

Backstage, there is a multitude of items that can be used as weapons such
as barrels, the motorcycle, and the forklift.

Around the Times Square area, you can use basically whatever you can find.
There's a helicopter with a rope ladder that you can climb and use the
helicopter to target your opponent with.  The helicopter will fire some-
thing at your opponent.  I'm not sure what it is yet.  Can't be bullets.

-------------------
| Backstage Areas |
-------------------

Just a brief note on backstage areas.  Many things can be used as weapons.
Some places can be used to help you a lot.  One such place is the parking
lot.  In the upper right area of the parking lot is a semi truck that has
a large container with the WWE logo on it.  If you manage to make it here
during your match, whip your opponent toward the container and they'll
automatically climb it.  Now, the computer, being the idiot that it is,
will walk back towards you.  But since your opponent's on top of the
container, they'll fall down, injuring themselves in the process.

Another thing to look out for backstage are large cracks in the walls.
Whip your opponent into these and usually they'll break, revealing a new
area.  One such place that I know of is in the locker room.  Break down
the wall to reveal the women's showers.

---------------
| Create Mode |
---------------

Create An Appearance
--------------------
If you wanted to create your own wrestler, this is where to start.  The
very first thing to do, of course, it to make your character.  In here,
you basically put on whatever you want on your character, give your
character a name, and distribute the attribute points to the different
attribute areas.  When you're done, you go to Create A Move Set to set
the moves you'd like your now completed superstar to have.

Create A Move Set
-----------------
This is where you edit superstars' moves, both existing and created.

Create An Animation
-------------------
You can create your own animations using this mode.  The different options
are fighting, running, walking, winning, and taunt.  So basically, you can
set the fighting style, running style, walking style, winning pose/style,
and taunts you'd like to see other than what is already selectable.

Create A Stable
---------------
Here, you can create tag teams, factions, or break up existing teams.

---------------
| Season Mode |
---------------

This is where you can unlock the items listed in Shopzone Merchandise.
THQ/Jakks Pacific created the SD$, or SmackDown Dollar.  Every time you
win or lose a match, you get SD$ and your superstar points raise or fall.
Your superstar points determine which of the titles you can compete for.
Also, a certain show determines which of the titles you can compete for as
well.  For example, all of the world title belts are on the Raw show, such
as the World Heavyweight Title, the World Tag Team Titles, and the
Intercontinental Title.  The majority of the WWE belts are on the
SmackDown! show, such as the WWE Title, the U.S. Title, and the WWE Tag
Team Titles.

You also earn a certain amount of attribute points for winning or losing
your matches.

The first time you start a season, you will be prompted to add or remove
characters that you would like to see appear during your season.  Then
you will be prompted to select which superstars you would like to have
on which show.  The next prompt will be for who you would to have as a
heel and as a face.  The final prompt will be for a superstar selection.

Please note that a season is one year.  For the game, it does not start in
January, but in April, which is right after the Pay Per View Wrestlemania.
The year ends in March of the following year.

During season mode, you have six options to choose from before starting
the show.  The first is move.  You can go around the different areas
backstage and talk to other superstars or to the General Manager of the
show.

The next is ShopZone.  This is where you can spend your SD$ to unlock the
different items listed there.  You receive SD% by winning or losing your
matches.  You earn more when you win them, and even more when you're a
title holder.

The next option is start the show.  Once you start the show, you can't go
back to the locker room until the next event.

After that is the attributes where you can increase your superstar's
attributes.  The five are strength, endurance, technique, and speed.
The max they all go to is 10.  It takes a lot of attribute points to reach
the max.  It may take somewhere between two to four seasons for most to
get enough points to max out every attribute for a character.

Profile comes next.  In this option, you can see who the title holders are
as well as see who are the contenders for the belts.

The last option is stable.  You can add and edit stables using this option
but you cannot create one during season mode.  To create one, you have to
go through the create option in the opening menu.

One last thing I'd like to mention about season mode.  If you get yourself
scheduled for a tag team match, you control all superstars on your team.

--------------------
| SmackDown! Meter |
--------------------

I'd just like to say a brief thing about the SmackDown! meter.  Who you
wrestle against determines the speed your SD! meter increases.  For an
example, let's say you were using Brock Lesnar and your opponent was Rey
Mysterio.  Because of the obvious differences in strength, weight class,
etc., Brock's meter would fill up at an alarming rate whereas Rey's meter
would hardly budge.

Keep this in mind when you select a wrestler for your season mode.  You
never know who you might get stuck wrestling against.

        Down + O: Turnbuckle Smash
        Left + O: Hanging In Reverse
        Up + O: Turnbuckle Smash
        Right + O: Turnbuckle Toe Kick

ROPE OPPONENT
    Rope Down
        O: Big Boot 6
    Rebound Attack
        Triangle, X: Flying Lariat 2
        Triangle, X: Running Leg Drop
        Triangle, X: Vaulting Body Press 2
    Jump Down Over
        Square, X: Vaulting Body Press 1

AERIAL
    Stand
        Left + X: Flying Clothesline 1
        Right + X: Double Axe Handle 3
    Down
        X: Elbow Drop 3
        Down + X: Elbow Drop 3
        Up + X: Elbow Drop 3

RUNNING
    Attack
        X: Clothesline 10
        Up/Down/Left/Right + X: Flying Lariat 2
    Grapple
        O: Irish Whip
        Up/Down/Left/Right + O: Turn Behind
        Groggy O: Neckbreaker 1
        Groggy Up/Down/Left/Right + O: DDT 1
    Back Attack
        O: Mat Slam 2
        Groggy O: Bulldog 2
    Squatting Attack
        Triangle, X: Elbow Drop 11
        Triangle, Up/Down/Left/Right + X: Running Leg Drop
    Counter
        O: Powerslam 1
        Up/Down + O: Back Drop 2
        Right/Left + O: Hip Toss 2

DOUBLE TEAM
    Stand
        O: Double Flapjack
        Down + O: Double DDT
        Left + O: Double Suplex 1
        Up + O: Double Clothesline
        Right + O: Double Beat Head
    Turnbuckle
        O: Irish Whip
        Down + O: Front Slam
        Left + O: Mudhole Stomping 1
        Up + O: Powerbomb 1
        Right + O: Kick To Gut

SPECIAL
    Special
        L1: Tombstone Piledriver Pin (facing opponent)
        Up/Down/Left/Right + L1: Chokeslam 8 (facing opponent)
    Favorites (done most of the time when controlled by computer)
        Sidewalk Slam 3
        Throat Thrust 8
        Batista Lifting & Toss
    Weapon Special
        L1: Guillotine 3
        Up/Down/Left/Right + L1: Guillotine 3
    Combination Moves
        1st X: Throat Thrust 1 (Reversed)
        2nd X: Undertaker Body Punch (Reversed)
        3rd X: Throat Thrust 4
